We further observe that geo-localization is strongly correlated with the other capability dimensions, suggesting that accurate localization depends on integrated perception, spatial reasoning, and commonsense inference rather than isolated visual recognition. Overall, ERGeoBench provides a unified framework for diagnosing and advancing human-like embodied geo-localization. Project Page: https://kaixuewen.github.io/ERGeoBench/

</details>


### [72] [Envisioning Beyond the Few: Disentangled Semantics and Primitives for Few-Shot Atypical Layout-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31266)
*Nan Bao,Yifan Zhao,Wenzhuang Wang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对布局到图像（L2I）任务在少样本非典型场景下面临的表征碎片化问题，提出了一种解耦语义与基元的表征驱动框架（DSP），通过语义锚定、基元注入和概念引导三个模块实现鲁棒的少样本自适应，在5-shot设置下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有L2I方法在少样本非典型场景下生成结果存在碎片化和扭曲问题（称为表征碎片化），其根源在于语义身份与视觉细节之间的粒度失匹配，导致两者纠缠不清。

Method: 提出DSP框架，包含三个核心模块：1）语义锚定（Semantic Anchoring）：将类别语义聚合成锚点以保持稳定的身份特征；2）基元注入（Primitive Imbuing）：建模可重新组合的基元以实现鲁棒的局部细节建模；3）概念引导（Conceptual Steering）：通过显著性感知目标函数引导优化，保持前景语义一致性。

Result: 在5-shot设置下，该方法在多个非典型领域上的视觉保真度和对齐度方面均取得一致性改进，优于现有最先进的L2I方法。

Conclusion: 提出的表征解耦策略有效解决了少样本L2I中的表征碎片化问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: The layout-to-image (L2I) task enables fine-grained control over image generation via object categories and spatial layouts. However, existing L2I methods yield fragmented and distorted generations under few-shot atypical settings. We term this failure as representation fragmentation, arising from a granularity mismatch that entangles semantic identity with visual details. To address this issue, we propose a representation-driven framework that disentangles semantics from primitives for robust few-shot adaptation. Specifically, Semantic Anchoring aggregates categorical semantics into anchors for stable identity, while Primitive Imbuing models recomposable primitives for robust local detail modeling. Conceptual Steering further regulates optimization with a saliency-aware objective to preserve foreground semantic consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in the 5-shot regime over state-of-the-art L2I methods in both visual fidelity and alignment across diverse atypical domains. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/iCVTEAM/DSP.

</details>


### [73] [SAM for Robust Mitochondria Instance Segmentation in Fluorescence Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31284)
*Suyog Jadhav,Dilip K. Prasad,Krishna Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过仅在合成荧光显微镜数据上微调SAM模型，解决线粒体实例分割中真实标注数据稀缺的问题，在真实数据集上取得了优于强基线的精度和Dice分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光显微镜下线粒体的形态分析对理解细胞健康至关重要，但现有基础模型（如SAM）因域差异（衍射限制分辨率、低对比度、复杂重叠结构）难以直接应用，且高质量人工标注实例分割数据集极度匮乏成为瓶颈。

Method: 通过仿真模拟真实线粒体数据并模拟荧光显微镜光学特性，生成大规模合成标注数据集，在此合成数据上对SAM进行微调。

Result: 在真实人工标注的荧光显微镜图像上，合成数据微调后的模型在精度和平均Dice分数上均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了仿真辅助训练在荧光显微镜实例分割任务中的潜力，为数据稀缺问题提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The morphological analysis of mitochondria in fluorescence microscopy (FM) is crucial for understanding cellular health, energy production, and metabolic regulation. While foundation models like the Segment Anything Model (SAM) have revolutionized natural image segmentation, their direct application to FM is hindered by a significant domain shift characterized by diffraction-limited resolution, low contrast, and complex overlapping organelle networks. Furthermore, the development of robust models is bottlenecked by a severe lack of high-quality, manually annotated instance segmentation datasets for mitochondria. In this paper, we propose a scalable solution to this data scarcity by finetuning SAM exclusively on synthetically generated FM data. We simulate realistic mitochondria data and emulate the optical properties of fluorescence microscopes to create a large-scale annotated dataset. We evaluate our fine-tuned model on a curated dataset of real, manually annotated FM images. Qualitative and quantitative analyses demonstrate that our synthetically fine-tuned model improves precision and average dice score over strong baselines. This work establishes the potential of simulation-assisted training for FM instance segmentation.

</details>


### [74] [DriveMA: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models with verifiable Meta-Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31271)
*Weicheng Zheng,Yixin Huang,Qiao Sun,Derun Li,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveMA提出了基于可验证元动作（meta-actions）的驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型框架，通过轨迹标注管道和基于规则的投影实现元动作的可验证性，并利用动作中心监督训练和回合级信用分配强化学习对齐高层决策与低层轨迹规划，在Waymo Open Dataset上取得了SOTA性能（2B模型Rater Feedback Score 8.060，4B模型8.079），并在NAVSIM上获得竞争力闭环规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶VLA框架面临语言-动作鸿沟（language-action gap），即语言指令与底层轨迹规划之间存在对齐困难，限制了端到端规划性能的提升。

Method: 1) 提出可验证元动作（verifiable meta-actions），将未来自车运动总结为紧凑的语言域意图；2) 构建基于轨迹标注的管道（trajectory-grounded annotation pipeline）从专家轨迹生成元动作；3) 通过基于规则的投影（rule-based projection）验证生成轨迹与元动作的一致性；4) 采用动作中心监督训练（action-centric supervised training）和数据高效的回合级信用分配强化学习（turn-level credit assignment RL），通过密集奖励和精确信用分配对齐高层决策与低层轨迹规划。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset视觉端到端驾驶任务上，2B模型达到8.060 Rater Feedback Score，4B模型提升至8.079，均达到SOTA；在NAVSIM上获得竞争力的闭环规划性能。

Conclusion: 即使是简单的元动作接口，只要具备可验证性并针对语言-动作对齐进行优化，就能实现SOTA的端到端驾驶规划性能。

Abstract: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models (Driving VLAs) aim to use language to improve end-to-end planning, but the language-action gap limits this promise. We propose DriveMA, a Driving VLA framework built on verifiable meta-actions, which summarize future ego motion into compact language-domain intentions and can be constructed from expert trajectories with a trajectory-grounded annotation pipeline and can be verified against generated trajectories through rule-based projection. DriveMA exploits this verifiability with action-centric supervised training and a data-efficient turn-level credit assignment reinforcement learning framework, explicitly aligning high-level decisions with low-level trajectory planning through dense rewards and precise credit assignment. DriveMA sets a new state of the art on the Waymo Open Dataset Vision-based E2E Driving, achieving a Rater Feedback Score of 8.060 with a 2B model and further improving it to 8.079 with a 4B model; it also obtains competitive closed-loop planning performance on NAVSIM. These results show that even a simple meta-action interface can achieve state-of-the-art planning when made verifiable and optimized for language-action alignment. Code, data, and models will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [75] [Authentication of Copy Detection Patterns via Cross-Camera Dual-Synthetic Referencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31292)
*Ivan Oleksiyuk,Roman Chaban,Slava Voloshynovskiy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于注册的跨相机双合成参考框架，通过深度学习方法同时利用数字模板和注册捕获图像生成高质量参考图像，用于验证复制检测模式（CDP），在异构移动相机上展示了更好的认证性能和对机器学习复制攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDP验证方法仅合成验证相机域的参考图像，忽略了打印变异性，导致验证性能受限。本文旨在同时解决打印随机性和相机畸变两大挑战，提高CDP认证的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出注册式跨相机双合成参考框架：每个打印CDP先由受控注册相机捕获，然后深度学习翻译器联合利用数字模板和注册捕获图像，为验证图像生成高质量参考。该方法基于信息论证明双参考比仅基于模板的参考包含更多信息。

Result: 在异构移动相机上的实验表明：认证性能提升，对基于机器学习的复制攻击具有鲁棒性，即使在小的CDP区域和低端设备上也能实现可靠的验证。

Conclusion: 同时处理打印随机性和相机畸变的双合成参考方法比仅处理相机效应的单参考方法更有效，信息论分析也支持了这一结论。

Abstract: Copy Detection Patterns (CDPs) are structures printed on physical objects to enable cost-effective authentication. Verification is achieved by comparing a captured image with the digital template from which the CDP was printed. In practice, printer stochasticity and camera distortions hinder this comparison, limiting robustness against counterfeiting. Prior work addressed camera effects by synthesising reference images in the verification camera domain, but it ignored printing variability. We introduce an enrolment-based cross-camera dual-synthetic referencing framework. Each printed CDP is first captured by a controlled enrolment camera, and a deep-learning-based translator jointly exploits the digital template and the enrolled capture to generate a high-quality reference for the verification image. We provide an information-theoretic justification showing that the dual reference is more informative than template-based references. Experiments on heterogeneous mobile cameras demonstrate improved authentication performance, robustness to machine-learning-based copy attacks, and reliable verification from small CDP regions and on low-end devices.

</details>


### [76] [TokTalk: Expressive Real-time Facial Animation from Audio-LLM Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31294)
*Qingcheng Zhao,Yifang Pan,Karan Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokTalk提出了一种直接从音频标记流实时生成3D面部动画的系统，利用Audio-LLM的音频标记信息，通过分块条件流匹配模型训练，实现了低延迟、高表达力的面部动画生成，在质量和控制方面优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式化身在面部表情和对话流畅性上仍显机械，主要因为采用语音识别、文本生成、语音合成、音频驱动面部动画的串行流程。作者发现Audio-LLM产生的音频标记已包含足够信息来重建合理的面部表现，因此希望直接利用音频标记生成面部动画，避免多阶段处理带来的延迟和表达力损失。

Method: 构建了一个音频标记到3D面部运动的新数据集；采用基于分块的条件流匹配模型（Chunk-based Conditional Flow Matching）进行训练；设计轻量级适配策略，使模型可无缝连接任何基于标记的Audio-LLM；分块处理机制实现了延迟与面部质量之间的参数化权衡。

Result: 消融研究验证了分块处理在延迟与质量间的权衡效果；实时性能在延迟上与先前方案相当；感知研究显示在质量、表现力和3D面部性能控制方面显著优于现有方案；展示了在聊天机器人化身、语音驱动用户化身和动画导演界面等多种应用场景中的灵活性。

Conclusion: TokTalk证明了Audio-LLM的音频标记可直接用于实时生成高质量、富有表现力的3D面部动画，避开了传统多阶段串联流程的局限性，且具有与各类标记化Audio-LLM兼容的普适性，为音频-视觉面部应用提供了灵活高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Audio-LLMs like GPT-4o have ushered in an era of conversational interaction with language models. Conversational avatars however, still seem robotic in facial expression and conversational flow, in part due to sequential stages of speech recognition, text generation, turn-based text response, speech synthesis, and audio driven facial animation. Based on our insight that audio-tokens produced by current Audio-LLMs carry sufficient information to reconstruct a plausible facial performance, we present TokTalk, a system that directly outputs expressive facial animation in real-time from streaming audio-tokens. We construct a novel audio-token to 3D facial motion dataset, on which TokTalk is trained using a Chunk-based Conditional Flow Matching model. A lightweight adaptation strategy allows our trained model to seamlessly connect to any token-based Audio-LLM at minimal computational overhead. Our chunk-based processing further enables parametric trade-off between latency and facial quality, shown through ablation studies. We further show that the real-time performance of TokTalk is comparable in latency to prior art solutions, and significantly favorable (via a perceptual study) in terms of quality, expressivity and control of the 3D facial performance. We showcase TokTalk's flexibility using a chatbot Avatar, a voice-driven user Avatar, and an animation Director's interface, as diverse audio-visual face applications.

</details>


### [77] [Learning from Fine-Grained Visual Discrepancies: Mitigating Multimodal Hallucinations via In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31312)
*Haolin Deng,Xin Zou,Zhiwei Jin,Chen Chen,Haonan Lu,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO)方法，通过多图像上下文的对比学习框架解决多模态幻觉问题。该方法包含三个关键组件：理论一致的对比优化目标（IC-VCO）、辅助正则化器（VCDist）和对比样本编辑策略。实验表明该方法在五个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态幻觉是视觉语言模型(VLM)的持续挑战。标准文本DPO因缺乏显式视觉监督而失效；现有视觉偏好DPO方法存在配分函数不匹配导致的理论不一致问题，且依赖粗粒度负样本可能引发捷径学习。

Method: 1) In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO)：将对比图像放入共享的多图像上下文中，确保目标函数数学严谨性；2) Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist)：辅助的可靠性门控正则化器，促进多图像对比训练与单图像推理的一致性；3) 对比样本编辑策略：通过精确语义扰动生成难负样本。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，IC-VCO取得最佳综合性能，样本编辑策略的有效性得到验证。

Conclusion: IC-VCO通过理论一致的多图像上下文对比优化框架，有效缓解了多模态幻觉问题，为视觉偏好优化提供了更严谨的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Standard textual Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) often fails to mitigate it due to a lack of explicit visual supervision. While existing works introduce visual preference DPO by contrasting original images against negative ones, they suffer from a theoretically inconsistent objective caused by partition function mismatches and rely on coarse-grained negatives that could enable shortcut learning. In this work, we propose In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO). By placing contrastive images within a shared multi-image context, IC-VCO ensures a mathematically rigorous objective. We further introduce Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist), an auxiliary reliability-gated regularizer that encourages consistency between multi-image contrastive training and single-image inference. Finally, we propose a contrastive sample editing strategy that generates hard negatives via precise semantic perturbations. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate IC-VCO's best overall performance and the effectiveness of our sample editing strategy. Code and data are available at https://github.com/OPPO-Mente-Lab/IC-VCO.

</details>


### [78] [DecMem: Towards Minute-Long Consistent World Generation with Decoupled Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31336)
*Zhenhao Yang,Xiaoshi Wu,Zhengyao Lv,Xiaoyu Shi,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Kwan-Yee K. Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DecMem，一种解耦的记忆架构，通过稀疏全局记忆和锚定局部记忆实现分钟级可控长视频生成，在细粒度时空一致性上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在长程推理下难以保持细粒度时空一致性，显式3D记忆和粗粒度帧级隐式建模均存在局限，朴素可学习记忆架构面临计算效率低下和注意力分散两大问题。

Method: 提出DecMem解耦记忆架构，包含Sparse Global Memory（稀疏全局记忆）实现高效细粒度全局历史访问，以及Anchored Local Memory（锚定局部记忆）实现稳定高质量外推。

Result: DecMem显著优于当前最先进方法，实现了分钟级可控长视频生成，具有高保真度和一致性。

Conclusion: 通过精准高效的长程记忆和优越的外推能力，DecMem为可控世界模型中的长视频生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generative models have promoted rapid progress in controllable world models. However, maintaining fine-grained spatio-temporal consistency under long-horizon reasoning remains a key challenge. In this work, we move beyond explicit 3D memory and coarse frame-level implicit modeling, and propose a fine-grained, learnable, and scalable memory for consistent world generation. We first identify two fundamental limitations of naïve learnable memory architectures in long-horizon extrapolation, namely computational inefficiency and attention dispersion. Through a systematic analysis of attention dispersion, we propose DecMem, a decoupled memory architecture that employs Sparse Global Memory for efficient fine-grained access to global history and Anchored Local Memory for stable and high-quality extrapolation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DecMem significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art methods. By ensuring precise and efficient long-term memory and achieving superior extrapolation capabilities, DecMem enables minute-level controllable long video generation with high fidelity and consistency.

</details>


### [79] [Triangle Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31419)
*Nicholas Fry,Eric Dexheimer,Kirill Mazur,Paul H. J. Kelly,Andrew J. Davison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个使用可微分三角形作为3D地图表示的密集RGB-D SLAM系统，通过在线可微分渲染三角形汤实现追踪与建图，并支持实时网格变形与碰撞检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅方法在新视角合成上表现优异，但三角形仍是传统渲染管线、游戏引擎及下游任务（如仿真、碰撞、编辑）的标准图元。此前离线方法已证明可通过Delaunay三角化优化非结构化三角形汤得到逼真网格，但尚无SLAM系统利用这一表示进行在线追踪与建图。

Method: 采用可微分三角形（三角形汤）作为地图表示，通过在线可微分渲染（Triangle Splatting）同时进行相机追踪和建图；地图可通过受限Delaunay三角化实时转换为连接网格，支持网格变形和碰撞检测。

Result: 在Replica和TUM-RGBD数据集上，该方法在3D几何指标上优于基线方法，相机追踪精度与现有方法持平，并实现了在线网格级场景编辑。

Conclusion: 可微分三角形作为SLAM地图表示在保持追踪精度的同时，显著提升了3D几何质量和在线网格操作能力，为密集SLAM系统提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: We present a dense RGB-D SLAM system using differentiable triangles as the 3D map representation. While 3D Gaussian Splatting has emerged as the leading method for novel-view synthesis, triangles remain the standard primitive for traditional rendering hardware, game engines, and downstream tasks requiring explicit geometry such as simulation, collision, and editing. Recent offline methods have demonstrated that an unstructured 'triangle soup' can be optimised into a photorealistic mesh via Delaunay triangulation across a set of posed images. Building upon this insight, we present the first dense SLAM system to employ Triangle Splatting to perform both tracking and mapping through online differentiable rendering of a triangle soup. The map can be converted into a connected mesh on-the-fly via restricted Delaunay triangulation, enabling new online capabilities such as mesh deformation and collision checking. On Replica and TUM-RGBD, our system outperforms baselines on 3D geometry, matches the camera-tracking accuracy, and enables online mesh-based scene editing.

</details>


### [80] [Feature-Optimized Vision for Adaptive 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31534)
*Eric Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应特征优化前端策略，通过综合评估纹理、重复性、区分性、三角测量角度和空间覆盖度为特征打分，为每视图分配特征预算，在保持广泛图像覆盖的同时提升三维重建质量，在合成多视图原型上优于随机、仅纹理和均匀网格基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定特征阈值和均匀特征预算在三维场景重建中容易在重复纹理、低视差区域或不稳定点上浪费计算资源，需要一种更智能的特征选择策略来提升重建效率和质量。

Method: 提出自适应特征优化前端，对候选特征从纹理、重复性、区分性、预期三角测量角度和空间覆盖度五个维度进行评分，为每视图分配特征预算以最大化有用的特征轨迹，在合成多视图原型上评估四种选择策略。

Result: 自适应策略在走廊、立面、物体桌面和杂乱场景中获得了最佳质量感知完整度和最低的重建RMSE，同时保持广泛的图像覆盖。

Conclusion: 所提方法并非替代现代学习匹配或神经重建系统，而是一种模块化的前端策略，可使经典和基于学习的3D管道更审慎地分配计算资源到视觉证据上。

Abstract: Three-dimensional scene reconstruction depends on local image evidence that is both visually discriminative and geometrically useful. Fixed feature thresholds and uniform feature budgets are easy to deploy, but they can waste computation on repeated texture, low-parallax regions, or unstable points. This paper proposes an adaptive feature-optimized vision front end for 3D reconstruction. The method scores candidate features by texture, repeatability, distinctiveness, expected triangulation angle, and spatial coverage, then allocates a per-view feature budget to maximize useful tracks under a fixed reconstruction pipeline. A small synthetic multi-view prototype evaluates four selection policies across corridor, facade, object-table, and cluttered scenes. Compared with random, texture-only, and uniform-grid baselines, the adaptive policy obtains the best quality-aware completeness and the lowest aggregate reconstruction RMSE while preserving broad image coverage. The result is not a replacement for modern learned matching or neural reconstruction systems; it is a modular front-end policy that can make classical and learned 3D pipelines more deliberate about which visual evidence they spend compute on.

</details>


### [81] [YARD: Y-Architecture Register Decoding for Efficient Hallucination Mitigation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31429)
*Ting Chen,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Yu Hu,Guan Huang,Mai Chen,Langsheng Lei,Jun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YARD是一种无需训练的Y型架构寄存器解码框架，通过在中间解码层分支并用寄存器令牌替换局部视觉令牌，在不增加额外推理开销的情况下有效缓解LVLM的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比解码方法存在两个问题：1) 完全丢弃视觉令牌过于极端，会引入语言幻觉；2) 损坏输入图像对视觉证据的控制粗糙，且需要两次完整前向传播导致高推理延迟。

Method: YARD利用中间解码层可靠的文本到视觉定位能力，在此关键阶段分支构建退化分支。退化分支将补丁级视觉令牌替换为寄存器令牌，保留全局语义但缺乏细粒度局部证据，形成图像感知但局部欠对比信号，且Y型架构避免第二次完整前向传播。

Result: 在生成式和判别式幻觉基准上，YARD在多个LVLM上达到最先进的幻觉缓解效果，同时显著降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: YARD通过巧妙的Y型架构设计，在无需额外训练的条件下，平衡了对比信号的有效性和计算效率，为LVLM幻觉缓解提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Contrastive decoding (CD) seeks to mitigate hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) by contrasting the output distributions of a standard model and a visually degraded model. However, existing training-free CD methods suffer from sub-optimal degraded branches: completely dropping visual tokens is too extreme and induces language hallucinations, while corrupting input images offers coarse control over visual evidence and suffers from high inference latency due to requiring two full forward passes. To address these dilemmas, we propose YARD, a training-free Y-Architecture Register Decoding framework. Motivated by the observation that reliable text-to-vision grounding predominantly emerges in the middle decoder layers, YARD constructs the degraded branch internally by sharing shallow-layer computations and branching exactly at this critical stage. For the degraded branch, YARD replaces patch-level visual tokens with register tokens, which preserve global image semantics but lack fine-grained local evidence. This image-aware yet locally under-grounded design provides a faithful contrastive signal without extreme modality mismatch, while the Y-architecture strictly avoids a costly second forward pass. Extensive experiments on generative and discriminative hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that YARD consistently achieves state-of-the-art hallucination mitigation across multiple LVLMs, alongside a significant reduction in inference latency.

</details>


### [82] [Astra: a generalizable report generation foundation model for 3D computed tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31437)
*Zhuhao Wang,Fang Chen,Chaohui Yu,Zihan Li,Yuchao Zheng,Jing Wang,Xuan Yang,Jia Guo,Zhenlu Yang,Xingju Zheng,Yihua Sun,Haojie Han,Xiaoxiao Qin,Zhan Feng,Wenbo Xiao,Chao Zhu,Yuehua Li,Shipeng Zhang,Hao Luo,Yunsong Peng,Fan Wang,Hongen Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Astra，一个基于90,678对胸腹部CT-报告对训练的可泛化CT报告生成基础模型。通过报告风格统一和强化学习诊断一致性优化，Astra在多个外部队列中实现了细粒度诊断指标平均44.1%的提升，并在临床实际中将胸部报告草拟速度提升29.6%，腹部报告完整性提升11.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT报告撰写耗时且高度依赖专家经验，现有自动报告生成方法缺乏支持多区域报告的通用基础模型，且不同队列间的报告风格和诊断术语不一致导致模型泛化能力受限。

Method: 1）构建包含90,678对胸腹部CT-报告的大规模数据集CTRgDB（涵盖8个器官系统的353,671个异常）；2）通过报告风格统一进行文本监督去噪；3）采用强化学习进一步优化诊断一致性，实现风格统一且诊断准确的报告生成。

Result: 在CTRgDB及6个外部队列上，Astra达到SOTA性能，细粒度诊断指标平均提升44.1%（P<0.001）；临床应用中，胸部报告草拟速度提升29.6%，腹部报告完整性提升11.3%（P<0.001）；作为基础模型可提升下游诊断性能并支持高质量报告合成。

Conclusion: Astra作为通用CT报告生成基础模型，兼具高泛化能力和临床实用价值，可作为广泛可及的临床辅助工具和下一代AI医疗的关键基础设施。

Abstract: CT interpretation requires radiologists to review hundreds of volumetric slices per examination, making reporting time-consuming and highly expertise-dependent. Automated CT report generation offers a promising route to improving clinical efficiency, yet the field still lacks a generalizable CT report generation foundation model that supports multi-region reporting and remains robust across external real-world cohorts. Intrinsic inconsistencies in reporting style and diagnostic terminology across cohorts make naive joint training prone to noisy textual supervision, thereby limiting model generalizability. Here we present Astra, a generalizable CT report generation foundation model trained on 90,678 thoracoabdominal CT-report pairs (CTRgDB) with 353,671 abnormalities spanning eight organ systems. By harmonizing report style and further refining diagnostic consistency via reinforcement learning, Astra achieves style-consistent and diagnostically accurate report generation across diverse anatomical regions and institutions. Evaluating on CTRgDB and six external cohorts, Astra achieves state-of-the-art performance with a 44.1% average improvement in fine-grained diagnostic metrics (P<0.001). In real-world clinical workflows, Astra assistance accelerates chest report drafting by 29.6% and improves abdominal report completeness by 11.3% (P<0.001). Furthermore, Astra also demonstrates broad utility as a foundation for CT AI development, improving downstream diagnostic performance and scaling vision-language pretrain through high-quality report synthesis. Overall, Astra serves as a broadly accessible clinical assistant and a pivotal infrastructure for the next generation of AI-powered healthcare.

</details>


### [83] [Vision-Language Models Suppress Female Representations Under Ambiguous Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31556)
*Arnau Marin-Llobet,Simon Henniger,Mahzarin R. Banaji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在性别模糊输入（如全副武装的工人、背影）下表现出的性别偏见。通过最小提示压力，模型倾向于输出男性性别，即使对高度女性刻板的职业也是如此。作者提出LALS（潜在关联倾向分数）指标，发现模型的内部表征与输出存在系统性解耦：内部编码女性关联却输出男性。逐层分析揭示了一种不对称过滤机制——男性信号从头至尾放大，而女性信号在中间层达到峰值后被抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注VLM在性别清晰可见时的偏见表现，但对实际中常见的模糊输入（如看不清性别的工人、背影）情况研究不足。作者希望揭示模型在模糊输入下的性别默认倾向，以及内部表征与输出行为之间的潜在差异。

Method: 提出LALS（Latent Association Leaning Score），一种零样本度量方法，通过将视觉token激活投影到模型的文本嵌入空间，逐token逐层测量概念关联强度。实验覆盖15个职业、800+张性别模糊图像、4种VLM，并进行逐层分析和颜色消融实验。

Result: 模型内部表征与输出存在系统性解耦：内部编码女性关联但输出男性。逐层分析发现男性信号端到端持续放大，女性信号在中间层达到峰值后被抑制。颜色消融表明服装颜色等文化负载视觉线索会进一步调节内部关联。

Conclusion: VLM在模糊输入下存在深层结构性偏见，即使内部已正确编码女性特征，输出层仍偏向男性。这种不对称过滤机制揭示了当前对齐技术仅修正表层输出，未能触及内部表征层面的偏见，需要更根本的去偏方法。

Abstract: Alignment teaches vision-language models (VLMs) to avoid expressing demographic biases, and when gender is clearly visible they largely succeed. Far less is known about ambiguous inputs (a worker in full gear, a figure seen from behind) cases common in practice yet rarely studied. We find that minimal prompting pressure exposes occupation-gender defaults when prompting ambiguous input images, with models collapsing to male even for strongly female-stereotyped occupations. But do these outputs reflect what models actually encode internally? We introduce LALS (Latent Association Leaning Score), a zero-shot metric that projects visual-token activations into the model's text-embedding space to measure concept associations per token and layer. Across 15 occupations, over 800 gender-ambiguous images, and four VLMs, internal representations and outputs are systematically decoupled: models often encode a female association internally yet output male. Layer-wise analysis reveals an asymmetric filter -- male signal amplifies end-to-end while female signal peaks mid-network and is suppressed before generation -- and a color ablation shows that culturally loaded visual cues such as clothing color further modulate these internal associations.

</details>


### [84] [VisionPulse: Dynamic Visual Sparsity for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31457)
*Hengbo Xu,Shengjie Jin,Yanbiao Ma,Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisionPulse提出了一种在推理过程中逐步修剪视觉令牌的框架，仅保留每步5%的视觉令牌，将推理轨迹缩短11.2%，同时几乎不影响准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型推理时的视觉令牌冗余导致计算开销大，且现有方法在预填充阶段静态剪枝，忽略了视觉证据在推理步骤间的动态变化。

Method: 通过轻量级视觉注意力质量估计每步的保留预算，利用其与有效视觉令牌使用的正相关性，仅保留最关键令牌，实现逐步视觉令牌剪枝。

Result: 每步仅保留5%视觉令牌，推理轨迹缩短11.2%，准确性几乎不变。

Conclusion: 视觉证据具有逐步依赖性，动态剪枝可有效减少冗余视觉上下文，自然缩短推理轨迹，提升推理效率。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large multimodal models (LMMs), inference-time overhead has become a key bottleneck for real-world deployment. Existing methods typically prune visual tokens at prefill, assuming the required visual evidence remains static during reasoning. However, we empirically show that visual evidence is strongly step-dependent: only a sparse subset of visual tokens is critical at each decoding step, and the critical set evolves across reasoning. Furthermore, we identify a coupled bottleneck where redundant visual context can steer the model toward query-irrelevant regions, lengthening the reasoning trace. Guided by these insights, we propose VisionPulse, a step-wise visual token pruning framework during reasoning. VisionPulse computes a lightweight visual attention mass to estimate the step-wise retention budget by exploiting its strong positive correlation with LMMs' effective visual token usage and retain only the most critical tokens under this budget. By enforcing visual sparsity during reasoning, VisionPulse filters redundant visual context while preserving relevant visual evidence, shortening reasoning traces naturally. Extensive experiments show that VisionPulse only retains 5% of visual tokens per step with reasoning traces shortened by 11.2%, while keeping accuracy almost unchanged.

</details>


### [85] [VolFill: Single-View Amodal 3D Scene Reconstruction with Volumetric Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31466)
*Tuan Duc Ngo,Chuang Gan,Evangelos Kalogerakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VolFill提出了一种基于混合3D VAE和潜在扩散Transformer的生成框架，从单张RGB图像重建完整场景3D结构（包括隐藏区域），利用几何基础模型的条件生成，在SCRREAM和NRGB-D数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张RGB图像重建完整场景几何结构（尤其是被遮挡的隐藏结构）极具挑战性，现有方法受限于逐射线约束或无结构点云查询，无法有效推断视觉证据不完整的区域。

Method: 采用混合3D VAE将稀疏截断有符号距离函数网格压缩为紧凑潜在空间，结合潜在扩散Transformer进行去噪以恢复完整场景；以几何基础模型为条件，利用丰富的空间先验进行鲁棒推理。

Result: 在SCRREAM和NRGB-D数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著优于当前基线，能够直接支持表面提取和大规模占用查询。

Conclusion: VolFill提供了一种结构化的场景表示，打破了传统像素对齐回归的局限，为整体空间理解提供了稳健基础。

Abstract: Reconstructing the complete geometry of a scene from a single RGB image remains challenging - especially when inferring hidden structures where visual evidence is incomplete. We introduce VolFill, a generative framework that predicts the 3D structure of the complete scene rather than relying on traditional pixel-aligned regression. Our method utilizes a hybrid 3D VAE to compress sparse truncated unsigned distance function grids into a compact latent space, paired with a latent Diffusion Transformer that denoises this representation to recover the complete scene. We condition the generation on geometry foundation models, leveraging rich spatial priors for robust reasoning. Unlike existing methods limited by per-ray constraints or unstructured point-cloud queries, VolFill provides a structured representation that supports direct surface extraction and occupancy queries at scale. Extensive experiments on the SCRREAM and NRGB-D datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms current baselines, providing a robust foundation for holistic spatial understanding.

</details>


### [86] [Enhancing Computer Vision Model Generalization in Warehouse Facilities: A Case Study on Anomaly Detection in Vertical Material Handling Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31487)
*Ruiliang Liu,Tina Dongxu Li,Joshua Migdal,Ken Meszaros,Trevor Dardik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简化仓库设施中计算机视觉模型部署的方法：仅在实验室环境中完成完整的模型训练流程（包括数据采集、标注、训练），通过优化摄像头布局、图像触发策略、模型选择与集成，使模型能泛化到不同仓库环境，从而将部署简化为摄像头安装、图像采集和模型部署三个步骤，省去新环境下的重新标注和再训练环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统仓库计算机视觉部署流程繁琐且重复——每个新环境都需要重新进行摄像头安装、图像采集、标注、训练和部署，资源消耗巨大。本文旨在通过实验室环境一次性完成完整流程，减少实际仓库部署时的重复性工作和资源开销。

Method: 聚焦垂直物料搬运系统的叉部异常检测，采用以下策略组合：优化摄像头安装位置、设计图像触发策略、精心选择模型并进行模型集成（model ensemble），在实验室环境中完成全部流程后，直接迁移至多种实际仓库设施环境进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过上述策略组合，模型能够从实验室条件有效泛化到多种不同的仓库设施环境，大幅简化了实际部署流程。论文明确指出此为实验性研究，非生产级部署。

Conclusion: 该方法有潜力变革仓库自动化实现方式——将部署简化为摄像头安装、图像采集和模型部署三个步骤，节省传统流程中图像标注和模型再训练所需的大量资源与时间。但仍需后续生产环境验证。

Abstract: Deploying computer vision models in Warehouse Facilities traditionally requires extensive resources for camera mounting, image collection, annotation, training, and deployment - a process often needing repetition in each new environment due to camera mounting constraints and environmental variability. This paper explores an innovative approach to streamline this process by conducting the standard procedure solely in a laboratory setting, focusing on vertical material handling systems and anomaly detection in forks of the systems. Through extensive experimentation, we have found that combining optimal camera placement, strategic image triggering, careful model selection and model ensemble enables effective generalization from laboratory conditions to diverse warehouse facilities environments, potentially transforming warehouse automation implementation by simplifying warehouse facilities deployment to just camera mounting, image collection, and model deployment, thereby saving significant resources and time typically spent on image annotation and model retraining. This is an experimental research study and not a production deployment.

</details>


### [87] [How can embedding models bind concepts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31503)
*Arnas Uselis,Darina Koishigarina,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型CLIP在多物体场景中的概念绑定问题，发现CLIP的嵌入存在可加性分解，但其绑定函数复杂度高，导致无法泛化到未见过的概念组合。通过在可控Transformer模型上训练，发现足够的数据覆盖能使模型学习到低复杂度的乘法交互绑定函数，从而实现系统性泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松判断在多物体场景中哪个颜色属于哪个形状，这种能力被称为概念绑定。而CLIP等视觉-语言嵌入模型虽然能识别单个概念，却无法表示哪些概念组合成哪些物体。作者希望探究CLIP在这方面的局限性是否本质。

Method: 论文首先从理论上分析场景嵌入的可加性分解性质，然后通过实验探究CLIP绑定函数的复杂度特征。接着，在可控的Transformer模型上从零开始训练，研究数据覆盖度对绑定泛化能力的影响。

Result: CLIP的场景嵌入可加性地分解为物体表示，这解释了为什么单模态探针能恢复物体信息。但CLIP的绑定函数复杂度高，阻碍了图像和文本编码器学习共享的绑定机制。而在可控Transformer中，足够的数据覆盖能产生低复杂度的绑定函数，其特征是概念间的乘法交互，从而实现系统性泛化。

Conclusion: CLIP在概念绑定上的局限性并非本质性的；通过充分的数据覆盖，模型可以学习到低复杂度的绑定函数，实现对未见过的概念组合的系统性泛化。

Abstract: Humans easily determine which color belongs to which shape in multi-object scenes, an ability known as concept binding. Vision-language embedding models such as CLIP struggle with binding: they recognize individual concepts but fail to represent which concepts form which objects. Although CLIP behaves like a bag-of-concepts model in cross-modal retrieval, object information is recoverable from its image and text embeddings separately. We study this tension through the binding function, which maps concepts to scene embeddings. We find that scene embeddings decompose additively into object representations, explaining why uni-modal probes can recover object information. However, CLIP's binding function is high-complexity, which likely prevents the image and text encoders from learning a shared binding mechanism that generalizes to unseen concept combinations. We then ask whether this limitation is fundamental. We show that it is not. In controlled transformer models trained from scratch, binding generalization emerges with sufficient data coverage. These models learn low-complexity binding functions characterized by multiplicative interactions between concepts, enabling systematic generalization. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/oshapio/binding-concepts-complexity.

</details>


### [88] [TunerDiT: Training-free Progressive Steering of Diffusion Transformer for Multi-Event Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31590)
*Ruotong Liao,Guowen Huang,Qing Cheng,Guangyao Zhai,Lei Zhang,Xun Xiao,Thomas Seidl,Daniel Cremers,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TunerDiT，一种无需额外训练、基于扩散DiT去噪轨迹中内在转折点观察的渐进式引导方法，通过事件分区掩码和跨事件提示融合两个手柄，实现多事件文本到视频生成，在8项指标上达到最优，并随事件数量增加展现扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V模型在生成长视频（包含多个事件）时面临挑战，缺乏有效的训练无关方法来控制多事件时序逻辑和视觉一致性。

Method: (1) 发现DiT去噪轨迹中存在内在转折点（去噪早期控制全局布局，后期控制细粒度细节）；(2) 提出Event-Partitioned Masking强制事件边界并允许跨事件过渡带；(3) 提出Cross-Event Prompt Fusion在后期注入相邻事件语义进行细化；(4) 构建Meve多事件生成评测基准。

Result: 在8项指标上达到SOTA，优于其他训练无关方法；文本对齐改善随事件数增加而提升，展现扩展性；可在视频一致性与事件分离之间可调平衡。

Conclusion: TunerDiT是一种简单有效、无需训练的多事件T2V生成方法，揭示了DiT去噪过程中的转折点特性，并证明了该方法随事件数量增加具有扩展潜力。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation faces challenging questions when generating videos with long horizons containing multiple events. Inspired by the intrinsics of the diffusion process, we probe video diffusion transformers (DiTs) and uncover intrinsic turning points in the DiT denoising trajectory where conditioning text affects generation from global layout to fine-grained details. Building on this finding, we present TunerDiT, a simple yet effective progressive steering method that requires no additional training for multi-event generation. TunerDiT comprises two steering handles: (1) Event-Partitioned Masking that enforces event boundaries while allowing cross-event transition bands; (2) Cross-Event Prompt Fusion that injects neighboring event semantics for late-stage refinement. We contribute a self-curated prompt suite for benchmarking multi-event generation, i.e., Meve. TunerDiT achieves state-of-the-art performance across 8 metrics and offers a tunable trade-off between video consistency and event separation, compared with other training-free methods. The improvement in text alignment increases with the event count, indicating a scaling possibility with increasing event count.

</details>


### [89] [Internalizing Temporal Consistency in Video Object-Centric Learning without Explicit Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31508)
*Rongzhen Zhao,Zhiyuan Li,Juho Kannala,Joni Pajarinen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出xSSC范式，通过通道分解（CCD）和跨时重建（CTR）两种机制取代传统的Slot-Slot对比损失，在不增加额外损失函数的情况下隐式地在视频OCL模型中实现时间一致性，显著提升了训练效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA视频OCL方法依赖Slot-Slot对比（SSC）损失来保持对象跨帧一致性，但SSC需要帧间一对一对象对应关系且引入了额外损失函数。遵循奥卡姆剃刀原则，作者希望将时间一致性作为隐式模型设计而非显式损失来强制实现。

Method: 提出xSSC框架，包含两个协同机制：（1）Chrono-Channel Decomposition (CCD) 将slot表示沿通道维度分解为静态和动态子空间；（2）Cross-Temporal Reconstruction (CTR) 通过融合当前帧静态通道与目标帧动态通道来随机重建目标特征，仅使用标准OCL解码器最小化重建误差。

Result: 集成xSSC到主流基线后，不仅提升了训练效率，还在视频对象发现和识别任务上取得了新的SOTA结果。PCA和梯度分析证明对象的时间不变语义和时间变化运动学被编码到不同的子空间中。

Conclusion: xSSC成功证明了可以通过隐式模型设计替代显式对比损失来实现时间一致性，为视频OCL提供了更简洁、高效的范式。

Abstract: Video Object-Centric Learning (OCL) aims to represent objects as \textit{slot} vectors and maintain their consistency across frames. Slot-Slot Contrastive (SSC) loss has become the cornerstone for state-of-the-art (SOTA) video OCL methods. While highly effective, SSC relies on one-to-one object correspondence across frames and introduces an extra loss. Following Occam's Razor, we propose a paradigm shift: temporal consistency is better enforced as an implicit model design rather than an explicit loss. To elegantly exclude SSC (\textbf{xSSC}), we introduce two quasi-zero-overhead synergistic mechanisms: (\textit{i}) Chrono-Channel Decomposition (CCD) structurally disentangles slot representations along the channel dimension into \textit{static} and \textit{dynamic} sub-spaces, serving as an empirically unified information bottleneck; (\textit{ii}) Cross-Temporal Reconstruction (CTR) stochastically reconstructs target features of either the current or previous time step by fusing current slots' static channels and target slots' dynamic channels, using a single standard OCL decoder with minor training adaptation. Thereby, the slot sets inherently learn temporal consistency by minimizing the standard reconstruction error alone. Extensive experiments show that integrating xSSC into leading baselines not only improves training efficiency but also establishes new SOTAs on video object discovery and recognition tasks. Furthermore, our PCA and gradient analyses confirm that objects' time-invariant semantics and time-variant kinematics are encoded into the proposed sub-spaces. Our source code, model checkpoints and training logs are provided on https://github.com/Genera1Z/xSSC.

</details>


### [90] [Lumos-Nexus: Efficient Frequency Bridging with Homogeneous Latent Space for Video Unified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31603)
*Jiazheng Xing,Hangjie Yuan,Lingling Cai,Xinyu Liu,Yujie Wei,Fei Du,Hai Ci,Tao Feng,Jiasheng Tang,Weihua Chen,Fan Wang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lumos-Nexus 提出了一种两阶段训练高效的统一视频生成框架，训练阶段仅对齐轻量级生成器与理解模块，推理阶段通过渐进式频率桥接（UPFB）将生成任务交给高容量预训练生成器，在保证推理质量的同时显著提升视觉保真度。同时引入 VR-Bench 基准评估推理驱动视频生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于连接器的视频统一模型虽在指令驱动视频合成中表现良好，但将大型高保真生成器集成到统一训练循环中的计算成本过高，限制了可实现的视觉质量。因此需要一种训练高效的方案，在不牺牲推理能力的前提下提升视频生成质量。

Method: 采用两阶段设计：1) 训练阶段仅将轻量级生成器与理解模块对齐，学习接收推理驱动的语义控制；2) 推理阶段引入统一渐进式频率桥接（UPFB），在共享潜空间中逐步将生成任务移交给高容量预训练生成器，实现从粗到细的精细化生成，保证推理质量的同时产出高保真视频。同时构建 VR-Bench 用于评估推理驱动的视频生成性能。

Result: 在 VBench 上，Lumos-Nexus 在视觉真实感和时间连贯性上取得显著提升；在 VR-Bench 上展现出强大的基于推理的生成性能。

Conclusion: Lumos-Nexus 通过训练高效的解耦设计，有效平衡了推理驱动生成能力与视觉保真度之间的矛盾，证明了渐进式频率桥接策略在统一视频生成框架中的有效性。

Abstract: Connector-based video unified models have demonstrated strong capability in instruction-grounded video synthesis, but integrating a large high-fidelity generator into the unified training loop is computationally prohibitive, limiting achievable visual quality. We therefore propose Lumos-Nexus, a training-efficient unified video generation framework that facilitates the development of strong reasoning-driven generation capabilities while significantly enhancing visual fidelity. Lumos-Nexus adopts a two-stage design: 1) During training, only a lightweight generator is aligned with the understanding block to learn to take in reasoning-driven semantic control. 2) During inference, we introduce Unified Progressive Frequency Bridging (UPFB) to progressively hand off generation to a high-capacity pretrained generator in the shared latent space, enabling coarse-to-fine refinement and producing high-fidelity videos without compromising reasoning quality. To fill the gap in reasoning-driven video generation benchmarks, we introduce VR-Bench, which assesses a model's capability to translate inferred intent into coherent and semantically aligned video content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Lumos-Nexus achieves substantial gains in visual realism and temporal coherence on VBench, while exhibiting strong reasoning-based generative performance on VR-Bench. Code and models are available at https://jiazheng-xing.github.io/nexus-lumos-home/.

</details>


### [91] [SVI-Bench: A Dynamic Microworld for Strategic Video Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31529)
*Yulu Pan,Han Yi,Seongsu Ha,Md Mohaiminul Islam,Benjamin Zhang,Lorenzo Torresani,Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SVI-Bench基准，利用团队体育作为动态微观世界，评估视频智能从感知到因果推理、模拟仿真到战略规划的完整能力栈。基准包含约3.5万小时广播视频、1500万标注动作等大规模数据，组织成9项任务，涵盖四个递进层级。实验发现模型在感知任务上表现尚可（~73%），但在高层级认知任务上急剧下降，最强模型在自主式任务中仅达5%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频智能评估存在两难：真实场景视频缺乏因果和战略问题的可验证真值，合成环境又牺牲了真实多智能体系统的复杂性。因此需要一个既能保留真实世界复杂性又能提供可验证标注的基准。

Method: 以团队运动作为动态微观世界，通过数据引擎将原始比赛数据转化为包含广播视频、动作标注、专家评论、比赛报告和结构化统计数据的密集交叉引用语料库，构建了从动态场景理解、因果推理、战略模拟到自主合成的四层递进评估体系。

Result: 模型在细粒度动作问答上达到约73%准确率，但在因果推理、战略模拟和自主合成任务上性能急剧下降，最强模型在自主式任务中仅达5%准确率，揭示了当前视频智能模型的认知能力断层。

Conclusion: 现有视频智能模型在高层级认知任务（因果推理、战略规划、自主决策）上存在显著能力缺口，SVI-Bench为未来研究提供了系统化的评估框架。

Abstract: True video intelligence demands more than recognizing what is visible: it requires reasoning about why events unfold, predicting what would change under different conditions, and deciding what to do next. We refer to this progression, from perception through causal reasoning and simulation to strategic planning, as Strategic Video Intelligence (SVI). No existing benchmark evaluates this capability stack: in-the-wild videos lack verifiable ground truth for causal and strategic questions, while synthetic environments sacrifice the complexity of real multi-agent systems. To bridge this gap, we introduce SVI-Bench, a large-scale benchmark that leverages team sports as a dynamic microworld, combining the complexity of real-world multi-agent interaction (10-22 agents making coordinated decisions under adversarial pressure) with the verifiability of explicit rules and definitive outcomes. SVI-Bench comprises approximately 35K hours of broadcast video, 15M annotated actions, 15K hours of expert commentary, 23K game reports, and 103K structured statistical records across basketball, soccer, and hockey, all constructed via a data engine that transforms raw game data into a dense, cross-referenced corpus. We organize evaluation into 9 tasks spanning a progressive four-pillar hierarchy: Dynamic Scene Understanding, Causal Reasoning, Strategic Simulation, and Agentic Synthesis. Evaluating strong multimodal and agentic baselines, we find a capability cliff: models perform competently on perceptual tasks, achieving approximately 73% on fine-grained action QA, but degrade sharply at each successive cognitive level. Agentic tasks prove hardest: the strongest model achieves only 5% accuracy when required to autonomously gather and integrate evidence across a corpus of 1.8M clips.

</details>


### [92] [nuReasoning: A Reasoning-Centric Dataset and Benchmark for Long-Tail Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31572)
*Zhiyu Huang,Johnson Liu,Rui Song,Zewei Zhou,Ruining Yang,Yun Zhang,Tianhui Cai,Hanyin Zhang,Mingxuan Gao,Valeria Xu,Jiali Chen,Yishan Shen,Yiluan Guo,Tony,Qi,Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出nuReasoning，一个面向长尾驾驶场景的大规模真实世界推理数据集与基准，包含20,000个20秒长的视频片段，涵盖空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理标注，支持推理评估与规划评估，实验表明推理监督能显著提升VLM的驾驶问答能力和VLA的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集和基准主要关注感知、预测或规划任务，缺乏对长尾驾驶场景中推理能力的充分监督。自动驾驶需要在复杂场景中运用常识、理解空间关系、推断智能体交互并做出安全决策，因此亟需一个以推理为中心的自动驾驶数据集。

Method: 延续nuScenes和nuPlan的数据体系，收集了来自多个城市的20,000个20秒长的驾驶片段，提供同步多摄像头图像、LiDAR数据、高清地图、目标标注以及人工验证的推理标注（包括空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理）。同时支持推理评估和规划评估，可直接研究推理监督对驾驶性能的影响。

Result: 实验表明，在nuReasoning上微调VLM显著提升了驾驶特定问答的性能；将推理监督融入VLA训练后，即使在推理时禁用文本推理输出，规划性能也有所提升。

Conclusion: nuReasoning为评估和提升鲁棒、可解释、以推理为驱动的自动驾驶系统在真实长尾场景中的表现建立了基础。

Abstract: Reasoning is essential for autonomous driving (AD) in long-tail scenarios, where vehicles must apply commonsense knowledge, understand spatial relations, infer agent interactions, and make safe decisions. However, existing AD datasets and benchmarks mainly target perception, prediction, or planning, and provide limited supervision for reasoning over realistic long-tail driving scenes. We introduce nuReasoning, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for reasoning-centric AD. Following the lineage of nuScenes and nuPlan, nuReasoning advances real-world AD datasets and benchmarks toward reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. The dataset contains 20,000 clips, each 20 seconds long, collected across multiple cities, with synchronized multi-camera images, LiDAR data, HD maps, object annotations, and human-verified reasoning annotations spanning Spatial Reasoning, Decision Reasoning, and Counterfactual Reasoning. Unlike prior datasets that focus primarily on visual question answering, nuReasoning supports both reasoning evaluation and planning evaluation, enabling a direct study of how reasoning supervision affects driving performance. Experiments show that fine-tuning VLMs on nuReasoning substantially improves driving-specific question answering, while incorporating reasoning supervision into VLA training improves planning performance even when textual reasoning outputs are disabled at inference time. These results establish nuReasoning as a foundation for evaluating and improving robust, interpretable, reasoning-driven AD systems in realistic long-tail settings.

</details>


### [93] [Joint Multi-Camera LiDAR Extrinsic Calibration via Learned Pairwise Initialization and Geometric Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31576)
*Aziz Al-Najjar,Marzieh Amini,James R. Green,Felix Kwamena*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合多相机-激光雷达外参标定框架，先独立初始化每台相机的位姿，再通过联合光束法平差实现全局一致性。在KITTI上平移误差0.89cm、旋转误差0.038°，在Walkley上平移误差从108.6cm降至3.1cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习标定方法独立处理每个相机-激光雷达对，忽略了多相机平台中相机间的刚性几何耦合，导致各相机标定结果在系统层面不一致。

Method: 两阶段框架：（1）对每个相机独立应用CMRNext进行初始外参估计并生成稠密2D-3D匹配；（2）通过多帧光束法平差联合优化，包含重投影误差、单相机先验项和相对位姿先验项，将独立预测转化为全局一致的多相机标定结果。

Result: KITTI上平移误差0.89cm、旋转误差0.038°；Walkley上平移误差从108.6cm降至3.1cm，显著提升了跨相机一致性和单相机精度。

Conclusion: 显式建模多相机间的刚性耦合能有效提升标定的全局一致性，尤其在单相机预测不可靠的场景下效果尤为突出。

Abstract: Most learning-based camera-LiDAR calibration methods treat each camera-LiDAR pair independently, ignoring the rigid geometric coupling in multi-camera platforms. As a result, per-camera estimates may be individually accurate yet inconsistent at the system level. We present a two-stage framework for joint multi-camera LiDAR extrinsic calibration that combines learned pairwise matching with geometric refinement. First, CMRNext is applied independently to each camera to produce initial extrinsic estimates and dense 2D-3D correspondences. These predictions are then jointly refined through a multi-frame bundle adjustment with reprojection, per-camera prior, and relative-pose prior terms. This approach converts pairwise predictions into a globally consistent multi-camera calibration. Experiments on KITTI (in-domain for CMRNext) and Walkley (out-of-domain) datasets show improved per-camera accuracy and inter-camera consistency. On KITTI, the method achieves 0.89 cm translation error and 0.038 rotation error. On Walkley, it reduces translation error from 108.6 cm to 3.1 cm, highlighting the benefit of explicit multi-camera coupling when single-camera predictions are less reliable.

</details>


### [94] [SurGe: Improved Surface Geometry in Point Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31577)
*Karim Knaebel,Gonzalo Martin Garcia,Christian Schmidt,Ilya Fradlin,Lucas Nunes,Daan de Geus,Bastian Leibe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出点图法向量指标以显式评估局部表面几何误差，并设计点梯度匹配损失和邻域注意力解码器（NAD）来改善前馈3D重建中的局部几何质量。SurGe模型在8个零样本单目几何基准上取得了最优平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建方法虽能预测全局3D几何，但局部表面几何不准确的问题在常规指标中反映微弱，需要更明确的评估方法和相应的改进手段。

Method: 1）提出点图法向量指标（point map normal metric），通过邻域3D预测评估局部表面朝向；2）设计点梯度匹配损失（point gradient matching loss），监督深度归一化的3D有限差分；3）提出邻域注意力解码器（Neighborhood Attention Decoder, NAD），渐进式上采样特征并利用邻域注意力进行局部特征混合。

Result: 在8个零样本单目几何基准上，SurGe在全局点图AbsRel指标上取得最佳平均排名，并在局部点图和点图法向量评估上实现一致改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的指标和模型有效弥补了现有方法在局部表面几何上的不足，验证了显式局部几何监督和邻域注意力机制的有效性。

Abstract: Recent feedforward 3D reconstruction methods predict point maps and estimate global 3D geometry remarkably well. However, their predictions still exhibit inaccurate local surface geometry, which is clearly visible qualitatively but only weakly reflected in common metrics. To make these errors more explicit in evaluation, we introduce a point map normal metric that evaluates the local surface orientation induced by neighboring 3D predictions. To reduce these errors, we propose two complementary components: a point gradient matching loss that supervises depth-normalized 3D finite differences, and a Neighborhood Attention Decoder (NAD) that progressively upsamples features and uses Neighborhood Attention for local feature mixing. Across eight zero-shot monocular geometry benchmarks, our model, SurGe, achieves the best average rank for global point map AbsRel and consistently improves local point map and point map normal evaluations.

</details>


### [95] [Recognizing Co-Speech Gestures in-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31589)
*Sindhu B Hegde,K R Prajwal,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态模型难以捕捉语义性共语手势的问题，本文提出了GRW数据集——首个大规模、帧级精确标注、覆盖150个词汇类别的手势识别基准，并在此基础上训练模型完成语义分类、词汇识别和时序定位三项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型难以捕捉与语音语义相关的共语手势，主要原因在于缺乏精确标注的训练数据。

Method: 构建GRW数据集（156,688个手工标注视频，涵盖150个词汇类别），训练视频模型完成三项任务：(a)手势语义性分类、(b)手势对应词汇识别、(c)手势时序定位。

Result: 构建了GRW大规模基准数据集，并建立了三项任务的评估基准。

Conclusion: GRW数据集为语义共语手势的识别与定位提供了标准化基准，有望推动多模态模型对该任务的理解能力。

Abstract: While humans naturally gesture during speech, only a sparse subset of these movements are visually depictive and semantically linked to specific spoken words. Current multimodal models struggle to capture these semantic co-speech gestures, heavily bottlenecked by a lack of precisely annotated training data. To address this, we introduce the Gesture Recognition in the Wild (GRW) dataset, the first large-scale benchmark designed to map unconstrained human gestures to specific words with frame-accurate temporal boundaries. Comprising 156,688 manually annotated video clips, GRW spans a highly diverse 150-word taxonomy of physical actions, spatial descriptors, and abstract concepts. We leverage GRW to train video models to (a) classify gestures as semantic or not, (b) recognize the word corresponding to a co-speech gesture, and (c) temporally localize the gesture. We also use GRW to establish benchmarks for these three tasks.

</details>


### [96] [Learning Global Motion with Compact Gaussians for Feed-Forward 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31595)
*Mungyeom Kim,Minkyeong Jeon,Honggyu An,Jaewoo Jung,Hyuna Ko,Jisang Han,Hyeonseo Yu,Donghwan Shin,Sunghwan Hong,Takuya Narihira,Kazumi Fukuda,Yuki Mitsufuji,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C4G是一个前馈式4D重建框架，使用紧凑的时间戳条件可学习高斯查询令牌，实现全局一致的运动建模，无需逐场景优化，同时引入视频扩散模型增强渲染，支持新视角合成、点跟踪和动态场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于前馈方法从单目视频重建动态场景时，逐帧预测3D高斯会导致高斯重复和视角依赖偏差，阻碍对场景运动的有效学习。

Method: 提出C4G框架，采用紧凑的时间戳条件可学习高斯查询令牌，每个令牌聚合全时间上下文的特征并解码3D高斯，高斯位置由目标时间戳调制。引入视频扩散模型增强渲染模块，并扩展特征提升至4D特征场。

Result: 在显著减少高斯数量的情况下实现强新视角合成性能，无需相机位姿，展现出更强的运动建模能力和对大时间间隔的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: C4G通过紧凑的高斯查询令牌和视频扩散增强，为前馈式动态场景重建提供了一种高效、鲁棒的解决方案，支持多种下游任务。

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular video remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Existing feed-forward methods predict 3D Gaussians pixel-wise for each frame, suffering from duplicated Gaussians and view-dependent biases that hinder effective learning of scene motion. We present C4G, a feed-forward 4D reconstruction framework built upon a compact set of timestamp-conditioned learnable Gaussian query tokens. Each token aggregates corresponding features across the full temporal context and decodes a 3D Gaussian whose position is modulated by the target timestamp, enabling globally coherent motion modeling without per-scene optimization. To capture fine-grained details, we further introduce a video diffusion model-based rendering enhancement module. Since our framework effectively aggregates features into Gaussians, we extend this capability to feature lifting, producing a 4D feature field that supports point tracking and dynamic scene understanding. C4G achieves strong novel-view synthesis performance using significantly fewer Gaussians and without requiring camera poses, while exhibiting stronger motion modeling and robustness to large temporal gaps.

</details>


### [97] [KLIP: localized distribution shift detection via KL-divergence with diffusion priors in Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31596)
*Alireza Kheirandish,Jihoon Hong,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于KL散度的OOD检测指标，利用扩散先验与后验分布之间的差异，无需校准数据或移位分布知识，即可在逆问题中检测整图或局部OOD，实验证明能检测肝脏CT中肿瘤等细微语义偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的OOD检测方法通常需要移位分布的先验知识，难以检测细微或局部分布偏移，且仅适用于全图像而非逆问题中的间接测量，亟需一种更通用、无监督的检测方案。

Method: 提出KLIP指标，计算扩散先验与后验分布之间的KL散度；无需校准数据或移位分布知识；支持整图OOD检测和图像内OOD区域定位。

Result: 能检测细微且语义上有意义的分布偏移（如健康肝脏CT与肿瘤CT的区分）；在不同扩散模型、数据集和逆问题场景下具有泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于KL散度的OOD检测指标在逆问题中有效，无需先验知识即可实现全局和局部检测，具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown promising performance as data-driven priors for computational imaging, as well as some capacity to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) images. However, existing approaches to OOD detection often require some knowledge of the shifted distribution, fail to detect subtle or localized distribution shifts, and operate on full images, rather than the indirect measurements available in inverse problems. We propose an OOD detection metric based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the diffusion prior and the posterior distribution, that (i) does not require any calibration data or knowledge of the shifted distribution, and (ii) can detect whole images as OOD as well as localize OOD patches within an image. Experimentally, we show that this metric can detect subtle yet semantically meaningful distribution shifts, such as the shift from healthy liver CT scans to those with tumors, and generalizes across different types of diffusion models, datasets, and inverse problems. Our code can be found at https://github.com/voilalab/KLIP.

</details>


### [98] [SOCO: Benchmarking Semantic Object Correspondence in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31597)
*Olaf Dünkel,Basavaraj Sunagad,Haoran Wang,David T. Hoffmann,Christian Theobalt,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SOCO是一个新基准，用于评估视觉基础模型在语义对象对应（Semantic Correspondence）方面的结构化目标理解能力。它包含100个类别、超过100万个对应点对，并引入对应类型分类法及关键点语言描述。实验发现：视觉骨干编码了强语义结构但跨类别迁移差；LVLM在文本引导定位上强于视觉参考跨图像匹配；对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测密集下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型评估缺乏一致性协议和细粒度部件级监督，难以衡量模型的结构化目标理解能力。语义对应（SC）虽能评估部件匹配能力，但缺乏系统性基准，尤其缺少对应类型分类和语言描述支持，使得对LVLM的细粒度部件理解评估受限。

Method: 构建SOCO基准：包含100个类别及超过100万对应点对，引入对应类型分类法（taxonomy of correspondence types），并提供关键点语言描述。利用该基准系统评估视觉基础模型和LVLM在语义对应任务上的表现。

Result: (i) 视觉基础骨干编码了强语义结构，但跨相关类别对应迁移差，且仅部分捕获目标部件位置；(ii) LVLM在文本提示的部件定位上强于视觉参考的跨图像匹配，揭示语言定位与细粒度视觉对应之间存在差距；(iii) 对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测分割、跟踪、3D姿态估计和3D检测等密集下游任务性能。

Conclusion: SOCO可作为评估视觉和多模态基础模型结构化、部件级表示质量的基准，其对应性能是比ImageNet分类更有效的下游任务性能预测指标。

Abstract: Measuring structured object understanding in vision foundation models remains challenging due to inconsistent evaluation protocols and limited part-level supervision. Semantic correspondence (SC) evaluates this capability by testing whether object parts can be matched across instances and categories under large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and geometry. To enable a systematic SC evaluation, we introduce SOCO, a new benchmark for Semantic Object Correspondence that introduces a taxonomy of correspondence types and provides consistent, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 categories and over 1M correspondence pairs. In addition, SOCO includes keypoint language descriptions, enabling the evaluation of large vision-language models (LVLMs) and their fine-grained part-level understanding. Comprehensive experiments reveal that (i) vision foundation backbones encode strong semantic structure but transfer correspondences poorly across related categories and only partially capture object-part position, (ii) LVLMs are stronger at text-prompted part localization than at visual-reference cross-image matching, exposing a gap between language-grounded localization and fine-grained visual correspondence, and (iii) correspondence performance predicts performance on dense downstream tasks, including segmentation, tracking, 3D pose estimation, and 3D detection, more strongly than ImageNet classification. Together, these findings position SOCO as a benchmark for structured, part-level representation quality in vision and multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [99] [Linear Scaling Video VLMs for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31598)
*Cristobal Eyzaguirre,Jiajun Wu,Juan Carlos Niebles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StateKV提出了一种推理时方法，通过固定容量的重要性感知循环状态携带跨帧上下文，将预训练的长视频VLM适配为线性时间视频预填充，在保持接近全自注意力精度的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频VLM在长视频和流式场景中面临自注意力计算与延迟随帧数二次增长的瓶颈，而现有效率方法（如激进帧/令牌丢弃或粗糙注意力近似）虽提升可扩展性但损失精度。

Method: StateKV采用双缓存架构：一个固定容量、基于重要性的循环状态用于跨帧上下文传递（实现线性时间预填充），另一个全量逐帧缓存用于解码阶段。无需微调或架构更改，直接适配预训练模型。

Result: 在3个长视频基准测试、7个模型（涵盖3个系列及多个规模）上，StateKV精度接近全自注意力，持续优于滑窗/基于近因的流式近似方法，并降低了视频预填充的FLOPs成本，可在固定预算下运行更大模型。

Conclusion: StateKV为可扩展的长视频理解提供了实用步骤，在不牺牲精度的情况下实现了线性时间预填充，且无需额外的训练或架构改动。

Abstract: Video vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in long-horizon and streaming settings, yet most video encoders still rely on spatiotemporal self-attention, causing compute and latency to grow quadratically with the number of frames. Existing efficiency methods improve scalability but often lose accuracy relative to full self-attention, for example through aggressive frame/token dropping or coarse attention approximations. We introduce StateKV, an inference-time method that adapts pretrained long-video VLMs to linear-time video prefill by carrying cross-frame context in a fixed-capacity, importance-based recurrent state, paired with a second full per-frame cache used for decoding. Across three long-video benchmarks and seven models spanning three families and multiple scales, StateKV remains close to full self-attention and consistently outperforms dominant sliding-window / recency-based streaming approximations, without fine-tuning or architectural changes. StateKV also reduces video-prefill cost measured FLOPs, enabling stronger accuracy at a fixed compute budget by running larger models. These results suggest a practical step toward scalable long-video understanding.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Protocol for evaluating ChatGPT in biomedical association generation and verification using a RAG-enabled, cross-model majority voting workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30400)
*Ahmed Abdeen Hamed,Luis M. Rocha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种评估ChatGPT生成疾病中心生物学关联能力的协议，利用生物医学本体和文献验证关联，并通过RAG增强的语义验证工作流检测LLM幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型（如ChatGPT）在生成疾病-生物学关联方面的可靠性，并解决其幻觉问题，确保生成内容的科学准确性。

Method: 1) 设计协议生成疾病中心生物学关联；2) 利用生物医学本体验证实体；3) 通过文献验证关联；4) 引入自一致性策略评估生成可靠性；5) 针对本体精确匹配的局限性，使用RAG增强的开源LLM进行语义验证。

Result: 通过RAG工作流实现跨LLM的语义验证，能够建立LLM生成内容的真实性标准并暴露幻觉。

Conclusion: 该协议提供了一种系统化方法，利用多种验证策略（本体、文献、自一致性、RAG语义验证）来评估和确保LLM生成生物医学关联的可靠性。

Abstract: We present a protocol to evaluate ChatGPT's ability to generate disease-centric biomedical associations. It outlines how we generate the associations, validate the biological entities using biomedical ontologies, and verify associations using literature. The protocol includes a self-consistency strategy to assess generative reliability across ChatGPT models. To address ontology exact-match limitations, we provide a use case performing semantic verification through a workflow enabled by Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) powered by open-source large language models (LLMs). This enables LLMs to establish truth over content generated by other LLMs and expose hallucination.

</details>


### [101] [Exploring Autonomous Agentic Data Engineering for Model Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30407)
*Yujie Luo,Xiangyuan Ru,Jingsheng Zheng,Jingjing Wang,Yuqi Zhu,Jintian Zhang,Runnan Fang,Kewei Xu,Ye Liu,Zheng Wei,Jiang Bian,Zang Li,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出**自主智能体数据工程**（Autonomous Agentic Data Engineering）任务，让LLM作为自主数据工程师，通过端到端的数据策划流程驱动模型专业化。实验表明，GPT-5.2通过迭代式智能体驱动数据适配，使学生模型性能提升57.29%，揭示了自主数据工程的潜力与瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数据策划方法依赖人工设计的工作流，缺乏对LLM能否自主执行端到端数据工程流水线以实现模型专业化的研究。本文旨在形式化并评估LLM作为自主数据工程师的能力。

Method: 将数据视为可优化组件，设计智能体系统自主执行数据策划的规划、生成和迭代优化三个环节，以模型训练后的性能提升作为反馈信号，跨多个领域进行数据适配。

Result: GPT-5.2作为自主数据工程师，通过迭代式智能体驱动数据适配，使学生模型性能提升57.29%。实验验证了自主数据工程的可行性并揭示了其瓶颈。

Conclusion: 自主数据工程可作为一项可衡量的能力，为智能体驱动的模型专业化开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on general tasks, while often struggling to adapt to specialized domains without high-quality domain-specific data. Existing LLM-based data curation methods primarily rely on human-designed workflows, leaving it unexamined whether LLMs can autonomously execute an end-to-end data engineering pipeline for model specialization. We formalize \textbf{Autonomous Agentic Data Engineering}, a novel task designed to evaluate LLMs as autonomous data engineers that drive model specialization through end-to-end data curation. We frame data as an optimizable component and study agents that plan, generate, and iteratively optimize training data across multiple domains, guided by post-training performance improvement. Experiments show that autonomous LLM data engineers yield substantial gains, as GPT-5.2 constructs a training curriculum that improves a student model by \textbf{57.29\%}, entirely through iterative, agent-driven data adaptation. By illuminating both potential and bottlenecks, our study establishes autonomous data engineering as a measurable capability and charts a path toward agent-driven model specialization\footnote{Code will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/DataAgent.}.

</details>


### [102] [Domain Adaptation and Reasoning Frameworks in Language Models: A Controlled Experiment with Historical Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30415)
*Francesco De Bernardis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过历史宇宙学作为受控实验环境，探究领域自适应如何重塑语言模型的解释行为。阶段一从头训练一个小模型于前哥白尼语料库（移除日心说相关内容），评估其是否仍会产生地球运动或日心说续文。阶段二使用QLoRA对大规模预训练模型进行微调，分析领域自适应如何改变解释框架和宇宙学立场。结果表明：阶段一的小模型偶尔产生局部地球运动续文但全局不稳定；阶段二的微调显著推动了向近代前解释框架的偏移，而宇宙学立场在框架内相对稳定。日心说输出的增加主要源于解释体系的再分布而非立场的直接修改。结论：领域自适应可能主要重塑生成续文的语言框架，立场的改变是这种偏移的次级效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究领域自适应（domain adaptation）如何影响语言模型的解释行为，特别是在历史宇宙学这一受控场景中，探究模型在接触特定历史文本后是否会表现出相应的宇宙观立场（地心说 vs 日心说）以及解释框架（近代前 vs 现代）的变化。

Method: 采用两阶段实验设计：阶段1从头训练小型语言模型于过滤后的前哥白尼语料库，评估地球运动/日心说续文的涌现情况；阶段2使用QLoRA对更大预训练模型进行微调。使用LLM-as-judge框架对模型输出的宇宙学立场（地心说、日心说、模糊）和解释框架（近代前、现代）进行标注与统计分析。

Result: 阶段1中小模型仅能生成局部、不稳定的地球运动续文，无法支撑连贯的宇宙学推理。阶段2中微调导致向近代前解释框架的大幅显著偏移，而条件宇宙学立场分布在框架内相对稳定。地心说输出的增加主要源于解释体系的再分布，而非立场的直接修改。

Conclusion: 领域自适应主要重塑了语言模型生成续文时所依赖的语言框架，立场的改变是框架偏移的次级后果，而非直接的目标行为变化。

Abstract: We investigate how domain adaptation reshapes explanatory behavior in language models using historical cosmology as a controlled setting. In Phase 1, we train a small language model from scratch on a pre-Copernican corpus from which explicit heliocentric references were removed, and evaluate whether Earth-motion or heliocentric continuations nevertheless emerge. In Phase 2, we fine-tune a larger pretrained model using QLoRA on the same corpus in order to study how adaptation modifies explanatory framing and cosmological stance. Model outputs are evaluated using an LLM-as-judge framework that labels both cosmological stance (geocentric, heliocentric, or ambiguous) and explanatory frame (premodern versus modern). In the constrained setting of Phase 1, the smaller models occasionally generate local Earth-motion continuations, but these remain globally unstable and insufficient to support coherent cosmological reasoning. In Phase 2, fine-tuning induces a large and statistically significant shift toward premodern explanatory framing, while the conditional cosmological stance distributions remain comparatively stable within those frames. As a result, increases in geocentric outputs arise primarily from redistribution over explanatory regimes rather than from direct modification of stance. These results suggest that domain adaptation may primarily reshape the linguistic frameworks from which continuations are generated, with changes in stance emerging secondarily from those shifts.

</details>


### [103] [Cross-Lingual Steering for Figurative Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30443)
*Linfeng Liu,Tiffany Zhan,Louie Hong Yao,Saptarshi Ghosh,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多语言大模型在生成比喻性语言时，存在跨语言可复用的内部信号，其方向可在不同语言间转移，且跨语言组合的方向能达到甚至超越目标语言自身的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多语言大模型生成比喻性语言的内部信号是语言特异性的还是跨语言可复用的，以揭示其内在机制的共享程度。

Method: 使用激活引导（activation steering）作为探针，从一种语言的比喻-字面激活差异中估计一个方向，并在另一语言的生成过程中应用该方向。实验涵盖5种比喻类别、6种语言和4个多语言大模型。

Result: 同一语言内引导效果可靠，隐喻和明喻最稳健；方向可跨语言转移，德语作为目标语言接收效果最好；多语言组合的方向可匹敌甚至超越目标语言自身的原生方向，而移除共享成分会削弱原生引导效果。

Conclusion: 存在跨语言可复用但依赖目标语言的比喻生成信号，为多语言模型共享的语义表征提供了直接证据。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models can generate figurative language, but whether the internal signals driving this behavior are language-specific or reusable across languages is unclear. Using activation steering as a probe, we estimate a direction for a figurative category from figurative--literal activation differences in one language and apply it during generation. Across five figurative categories, six languages, and four multilingual LLMs, these directions steer reliably within their own language, most robustly for metaphor and simile. More importantly, they transfer across languages: a direction learned in one increases the target behavior when applied to another, with German among the most receptive targets. Going further, directions assembled from other languages can match or even surpass a target language's own native direction, while removing this shared component weakens native steering. Together, these results provide direct evidence of a reusable but target-dependent cross-lingual signal for figurative generation.

</details>


### [104] [Can LLM Teams Play What? Where? When?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30459)
*Anastasia Kotelnikova,Viktor Byzov,Maria Dolzhenkova,Evgeny Kotelnikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于团队交互策略（投票、静默团队、健谈团队）提升大型语言模型在“What? Where? When?”问答游戏中的表现，发现团队策略显著优于单模型基线（最高提升20个百分点），最佳团队准确率达44.23%，接近人类水平。分析表明模型间分歧会降低准确率，但合理解释可缓解该问题；LLM团队主要发挥答案选择与错误过滤机制，而非生成新颖解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在需要间接推理、文化知识和协调假设测试的任务上表现有限，而团队协作在人类推理中能有效提升表现。本文旨在探索团队交互策略是否能改善LLM在这些复杂推理任务上的性能。

Method: 提出三种团队策略：Voting（投票）、Silent Team（队长仅观察最终答案）、Talkative Team（队长观察答案及推理过程）。在2025年发布的572道ChGK问题上，使用六种最新大型开源模型进行评估，并与单模型基线及人类表现进行比较。

Result: 团队策略显著优于单模型基线，最高提升20个百分点准确率；最佳团队（Talkative Team）达44.23%准确率，接近人类团队水平；模型间分歧强烈预测较低准确率，但沟通解释能显著缓解性能下降；未发现队长自我偏好偏见，同伴推理有助于提升队长判断。

Conclusion: LLM团队主要通过答案选择与错误过滤机制发挥作用，而非生成新颖解决方案；交互策略对提升多智能体系统性能至关重要，自适应策略是未来有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain limited on tasks requiring indirect reasoning, cultural knowledge, and coordinated hypothesis testing. We investigate whether team-based interaction improves LLM performance in What? Where? When? (ChGK), a quiz game designed to reward collective reasoning. We introduce three team strategies: Voting, Silent Team (the captain observes final answers), and Talkative Team (the captain observes both answers and rationales). To minimize data leakage, we evaluate these strategies on a dataset consisting of 572 ChGK questions released in 2025. Using six recent large-scale open models, we show that team-based strategies outperform single-model baselines, yielding gains of up to 20 percentage points in accuracy. The best team achieves 44.23% accuracy, and approaches human team performance on questions with available human statistics. Analysis of inter-model diversity reveals that disagreement strongly predicts lower accuracy, but explanatory communication substantially mitigates performance drops. We further examine captain behavior and find no evidence of self-preference bias; access to peer rationales improves captain judgments. Overall, LLM teams function primarily as answer selection and error-filtering mechanisms rather than generators of novel solutions. Our findings highlight the importance of interaction and suggest adaptive strategies as a promising direction for multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [105] [Knowledge Graph-Enhanced Zero-Shot Topic Classification: A Multi-Strategy Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30465)
*Shahana Akter,Yatharth Vohra,Ankita Shukla,Souvika Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究零样本多标签主题分类中知识图谱增强的影响，提出了包含四种变体的框架，并在15个LLM和8个数据集上测试。结果表明：关键词增强方法表现最佳，图谱增强对小模型有益但对大模型有负面影响，自一致性解码在所有实验中均未提升性能且计算成本增加约5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签主题分类在无标注训练数据的情况下是一个具有挑战性的任务，尤其是当文档包含复杂的关联关系信息时。本文旨在探索如何通过知识图谱增强来提升零样本多标签主题分类的性能。

Method: 提出了一个零样本多标签主题分类框架，包含四种变体：纯文章分类、关键词增强分类及两者的自一致性解码变体。在此基础上，通过类似KGGen的流水线从输入文档中提取基于主谓宾三元组的知识图谱，对每种变体进行图谱增强。在15个LLM和8个多标签数据集上测试了全部8种方法。

Result: 关键词增强分类（AK）是基础框架中表现最好的方法。15个LLM中有6个超过了句子编码器基线。图谱增强对小模型有正面影响，对大模型有负面影响。自一致性解码变体在所有实验中均未带来性能提升，且计算成本增加了约5倍。

Conclusion: 大模型通过预训练已包含足够的关联关系信息，因此知识图谱增强对其不再有益；自一致性解码在该任务中无效且效率低下。

Abstract: Multi-label topic classification without labeled training data is a challenging task, specially when documents contain complex relational information. We present a zero-shot multi-label topic classification framework and systematically investigate how per-article knowledge graph augmentation affects its performance. The base framework classifies topics in documents without labeled training data and has four variants: article-only classification, keyword-enhanced classification, and self-consistency decoding variants of both. Then, we augment each base variant with per article knowledge graph. This graph is extracted from the input document through a pipeline similar to KGGen based on subject-predicate-object triples. We test all eight methods, four base and four graph augmented on fifteen LLMs and eight multi-label datasets across different domains. For the base framework, keyword-enhanced classification (AK) is the best performing method, and six out of fifteen LLMs surpass the sentence-encoder baseline. Graph augmentation has positive and negative impacts on small and large models, respectively. This shows that larger models already contain enough relational information from pretraining. Furthermore, the self-consistency decoding variant does not show performance improvements in any experiment while increasing computation costs about fivefold.

</details>


### [106] [Your Multimodal Speech Model Says I Have a Face for Radio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30472)
*Maya K. Nachesa,Vlad Niculae,Vagrant Gautam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次评估了多模态语音识别中的偏见问题，通过将不同人脸与相同音频配对，发现mWhisper-Flamingo和Gemini模型在性别、种族及其交叉属性上存在高达4.05词错误率点的服务质量差异，表明增加模态未必更好，甚至可能导致偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单模态模型中的性能与偏见已被广泛研究，但多模态模型引入新模态后是否影响偏见尚不清楚。由于人类在多模态下会产生偏见，研究者希望探究这一现象在AI模型中是否存在。

Method: 创建将不同人脸（涉及性别、种族及其交叉类别）与相同音频配对的视频，测量语音转录准确率的变化，评估多模态语音识别中的服务质量差异。

Result: mWhisper-Flamingo和Gemini模型在不同人口属性组间存在显著的词错误率差异，最大差距达4.05个百分点，且性别、种族及其交叉属性均表现出偏见。

Conclusion: 开发者应优先评估、修复和沟通此类局限性，因为增加模态并不意味着更好，反而可能引入或放大偏见。

Abstract: As large neural models have become better at language tasks, researchers are increasingly building multi- and omnimodal models that handle more modalities of data. One example is the expansion of speech recognition models to audio-visual data for noise mitigation and multimodal subtitling. While performance and bias have been studied extensively in the single-modality regime, it is unknown how new modalities affect this, even though they produce biases in humans. We therefore propose the first bias evaluation of multimodal speech recognition, where we create videos pairing different faces with the same audio, and measure changes in speech transcription accuracy. We find large quality-of-service differences across mWhisper-Flamingo and Gemini models, with drops of up to 4.05 word error rate points, across self-declared gender, ethnicity, and their intersection. Our findings point to a priority for developers to evaluate, fix, and communicate such limitations, as providing more signals through additional modalities is not necessarily better, and may even lead to biased outcomes.

</details>


### [107] [When English Rewrites Local Knowledge: Global Narrative Dominance in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30481)
*Md Arid Hasan,Ruwad Naswan,Farhan Samir,Sharifa Sultana,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在低资源文化语境（孟加拉语）中的“全球叙事主导”失败模式。作者构建了CulturalNB数据集（717条孟加拉文化实例，含平行孟英QA对及元数据），评估了9个SOTA LLM。发现英语提问会系统性增加全球替代和制度框架，减少本地视角覆盖；本地证据虽能改善事实一致性，但无法消除语言引发的认知偏移。文化失败不仅是知识缺失，更是叙事根基与优先级的错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为跨语言知识接口广泛使用，但在回答文化相关问题时往往反映全球主导叙事而非本地语境。现有研究缺乏对低资源文化背景下这种失败模式的系统分析，特别是语言选择（英语vs本地语言）如何影响文化叙事的呈现。

Method: 构建CulturalNB数据集（717条孟加拉文化实例，含平行孟英QA对、支撑证据、元数据与社会文化标注）。采用纯问题提示和基于证据的提示两种范式，使用人类裁判和两个独立LLM裁判，评估9个SOTA LLM在跨语言一致性、语言锚定、全球替代、制度偏见和认识论视角覆盖等指标上的表现。

Result: 英语提问系统性增加全球替代和制度框架，减少本地视角覆盖。本地证据能提升事实一致性和视角覆盖，但无法消除语言引发的认识论偏移。不同LLM在文化敏感性上差异显著。

Conclusion: LLM的文化失败不仅是知识缺失（missing-knowledge errors），更是根基性失败（grounding failures）和叙事优先级错位（narrative prioritization）。解决文化偏差需要超越知识注入，关注叙事框架的重新平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used as cross-lingual knowledge interfaces. However, culturally grounded questions often reflect globally dominant narratives rather than local contexts. We study this failure mode as \textit{global narrative dominance} in Bangla, a low-resource cultural context. We introduce \texttt{CulturalNB}, a dataset of 717 manually curated Bengali cultural instances with parallel Bangla--English question--answer pairs and supporting evidence, metadata, and sociocultural annotations. Using question-only and evidence-based prompting, we evaluate nine state-of-the-art LLMs with human and two independent LLM judges across metrics for cross-lingual consistency, language anchoring, global substitution, institutional bias, and epistemic perspective coverage. Results show that questions asked in English systematically increase global substitution and institutional framing while reducing local perspective coverage. Local evidence improves factual consistency and perspective coverage, but does not eliminate language-induced epistemic shifts. These findings suggest that cultural failures in LLMs are not only missing-knowledge errors but also failures of grounding and narrative prioritization.

</details>


### [108] [Configurable Reward Model for Balanced Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30487)
*Zhengping Jiang,Mehran Khodabandeh,Akash Bharadwaj,Manik Bhandari,Mayur Srungarapu,Anqi Liu,Benjamin Van Durme,Li Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出可配置安全奖励模型(CSRM)，通过联合优化校准安全合规与奖励建模，结合配置目标数据增强，在可配置安全基准上取得SOTA性能，并显著提升LLM的有用性-安全性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令微调LLM和独立安全分类器难以泛化到新的安全配置，缺乏能根据动态变化的安全规范进行显式配置的奖励模型。

Method: 提出CSRM，联合优化校准安全合规与奖励建模；采用配置目标数据增强，强制指令遵循同时保持相对严重性结构。

Result: CoSApien上94.6% F1，DynaBench上75.8% F1；下游安全对齐中显著改善有用性-安全性权衡。

Conclusion: CSRM对细粒度安全配置和对话细微差别敏感，显著提升对未见安全配置的泛化能力，无需额外人工标注。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) to heterogeneous and rapidly evolving safety requirements remains a critical challenge. Existing instruction-tuned LLMs and standalone safety classifiers often fail to generalize to new safety configurations, motivating the need for Reward Models (RMs) that are explicitly configurable to changing specifications. We introduce the Configurable Safety Reward Model (CSRM), which is jointly optimized for calibrated safety compliance and reward modeling. Our approach is supported by configuration-targeted data augmentation that enforces instruction adherence while preserving relative severity structure. The resulting RM is sensitive to fine-grained safety configurations and conversational nuances, substantially improving generalization to previously unseen safety configurations. CSRM achieves state-of-the-art performance on recent configurable safety benchmarks, including CoSApien (94.6% F1) and DynaBench (75.8% F1), without requiring additional human annotation. When used for downstream safety alignment, CSRM yields LLMs with a significantly improved helpfulness-safety tradeoff compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [109] [CanLegalRAGBench: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Canadian Case Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30497)
*Ethan Zhao,Maksym Taranukhin,Wei Cui,Moira Aikenhead,Vered Shwartz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CanLegalRAGBench，一个基于真实查询和专家标注答案的加拿大法律QA基准，用于评估RAG法律助手。评估发现检索性能对设计选择敏感，开源嵌入模型可与闭源模型竞争，自动评估存在局限性，且生成答案中8-29%的声明缺乏检索文档支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律RAG基准多依赖合成查询而非真实法律场景，且加拿大法律在现有评估中代表性不足，LLM幻觉问题在司法领域尤为严重。

Method: 构建基于真实查询和专家标注答案的加拿大法律QA基准CanLegalRAGBench，通过检索性能和生成质量评估对主流模型进行系统评测。

Result: 检索性能对设计选择敏感；开源嵌入模型与闭源模型竞争力相当；自动评估会惩罚检索到替代相关文档的模型；生成答案常偏离标准答案，8-29%的声明缺乏检索文档支持。

Conclusion: 该基准有助于推动法律RAG系统在幻觉抑制和检索鲁棒性方面的改进，但现有自动评估方法仍需完善。

Abstract: RAG-based legal assistants have been growing in popularity, but LLM hallucinations remain a key issue and potentially undermines justice. While benchmarks have been developed to evaluate progress, many rely on synthetic queries rather than realistic legal scenarios. Moreover, Canadian law remains underrepresented in existing evaluations. To address this gap, we introduce CanLegalRAGBench, a Canadian legal QA benchmark based on realistic queries and expert-annotated answers grounded in case law. Our evaluation shows that retrieval performance is sensitive to design choices and that open-source embedding models are competitive with closed source models. However, it also reveals the limitation of automatic evaluations that penalize systems for retrieving alternative relevant documents. We also find that generated answers often diverge from gold responses, either with hallucinations or by producing overly detailed or irrelevant content, with 8-29% of claims not being supported by the retrieved documents. We hope this benchmark will help drive continued progress in addressing limitations of legal RAG systems.

</details>


### [110] [Linear Ensembles Wash Away Watermarks: On the Fragility of Distributional Perturbations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30501)
*Zhihao Wu,Gracia Gong,Qinglin Zhu,Yudong Chen,Runcong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个基础性漏洞：当用户可访问多个模型时，水印技术会失效。平均3-5个模型的输出概率分布即可消除独立的水印扰动，使检测z-score从5-300降至2以下，同时提升文本质量27.5%且运行速度快6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方案假设用户只能访问单一模型，但现实中用户通常可访问多个商业模型。作者旨在揭示这一现实条件下水印的根本脆弱性，并证明简单的模型输出平均化即可有效规避水印检测。

Method: 理论证明输出概率分布平均化可恢复未加水印分布（误差为二阶项）；提出WASH方法解决异构模型间的词汇对齐和分词差异等实践挑战；在6种水印方案和3种LLM上进行实验验证。

Result: 平均3个模型即可将检测z-score从5-300压制到2以下（检测阈值为4）；在5% FPR下TPR降至50%以下；文本质量提升27.5%；长序列生成速度比最优基线快6倍。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的AI文本水印检测要么接受这一根本性漏洞，要么需要模型提供商之间前所未有的协调合作。
